# Flecs Script compatibility

Generated by `tools/sync_flecs_script_tests.py`; edit statuses/evidence in `test/compat/flecs_manifest.json` and regenerate. No passing status is inferred from a build, an oracle result, or another dimension.

- Flecs baseline: `fa788c60c87b4effc1124027db86ef0f30a778d4`.
- SiECS baseline: `c41a4a220e2d6fabfa1c3cf7c0461647c5c765fd`.
- Coverage inventory: **34 suites, 4130 testcases, 8860 parameterized executions**.
- Statuses: `PASS`, `TODO`, `BLOCKED_CORE`, `BLOCKED_REFLECT`, `HOST_DIFFERENCE`.
- Phase 0 only: the SiECS Script runtime is not implemented. All language/API entries remain TODO. The candidate runner reports a gap and cannot pass parity.
- TODO in a dimension also covers applicability not yet audited; it never means PASS.
- Every non-TODO status requires evidence; HOST_DIFFERENCE additionally requires a precise justification. Test parameters are retained verbatim in the manifest.

Duplicate testcase registrations are retained with occurrence IDs (`#2`, etc.); counts reflect the upstream manifest, not deduplicated function names.
